1. using System;
2. using System.Collections.Generic;
3. using System.Threading.Tasks;
4. using SDKTemplate;
5. using Windows.Graphics.Printing;
6. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
7. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
8. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Printing;
9. 
10. namespace PrintSample
11. {
12.     public class PrintHelper
13.     {
14.         /// <summary>
15.         /// The percent of app's margin width, content is set at 85% (0.85) of the area's width
16.         /// </summary>
17.         protected double ApplicationContentMarginLeft = 0.075;
18. 
19.         /// <summary>
20.         /// The percent of app's margin height, content is set at 94% (0.94) of tha area's height
21.         /// </summary>
22.         protected double ApplicationContentMarginTop = 0.03;
23.         
24.         /// <summary>
25.         /// PrintDocument is used to prepare the pages for printing.
26.         /// Prepare the pages to print in the handlers for the Paginate, GetPreviewPage, and AddPages events.
27.         /// </summary>
28.         protected PrintDocument printDocument;
29. 
30.         /// <summary>
31.         /// Marker interface for document source
32.         /// </summary>
33.         protected IPrintDocumentSource printDocumentSource;
34. 
35.         /// <summary>
36.         /// A list of UIElements used to store the print preview pages.  This gives easy access
37.         /// to any desired preview page.
38.         /// </summary>
39.         internal List<UIElement> printPreviewPages;
40. 
41.         // Event callback which is called after print preview pages are generated.  Photos scenario uses this to do filtering of preview pages
42.         protected event EventHandler PreviewPagesCreated;
43. 
44.         /// <summary>
45.         /// First page in the printing-content series
46.         /// From this "virtual sized" paged content is split(text is flowing) to "printing pages"
47.         /// </summary>
48.         protected FrameworkElement firstPage;
49. 
50.         /// <summary>
51.         ///  A reference back to the scenario page used to access XAML elements on the scenario page
52.         /// </summary>
53.         protected Page scenarioPage;
54. 
55.         /// <summary>
56.         ///  A hidden canvas used to hold pages we wish to print
57.         /// </summary>
58.         protected Canvas PrintCanvas
59.         {
60.             get
61.             {
62.                 return scenarioPage.FindName("PrintCanvas") as Canvas;
63.             }
64.         }
65. 
66.         /// <summary>
67.         /// Constructor
68.         /// </summary>
69.         /// <param name="scenarioPage">The scenario page constructing us</param>
70.         public PrintHelper(Page scenarioPage)
71.         {
72.             this.scenarioPage = scenarioPage;
73.             printPreviewPages = new List<UIElement>();
74.         }
75. 
76.         /// <summary>
77.         /// This function registers the app for printing with Windows and sets up the necessary event handlers for the print process.
78.         /// </summary>
79.         public virtual void RegisterForPrinting()
80.         {
81.             printDocument = new PrintDocument();
82.             printDocumentSource = printDocument.DocumentSource;
83.             printDocument.Paginate += CreatePrintPreviewPages;
84.             printDocument.GetPreviewPage += GetPrintPreviewPage;
85.             printDocument.AddPages += AddPrintPages;
86. 
87.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#getforcurrentview
88.             PrintManager printMan = PrintManager.GetForCurrentView();
89.             printMan.PrintTaskRequested += PrintTaskRequested;
90.         }
91. 
92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// This function unregisters the app for printing with Windows.
94.         /// </summary>
95.         public virtual void UnregisterForPrinting()
96.         {
97.             if (printDocument == null)
98.             {
99.                 return;
100.             }
101. 
102.             printDocument.Paginate -= CreatePrintPreviewPages;
103.             printDocument.GetPreviewPage -= GetPrintPreviewPage;
104.             printDocument.AddPages -= AddPrintPages;
105.             printDocument = null;
106. 
107.             // Remove the handler for printing initialization.
108.             // TODO[migrate-002]: see PATTERNS.md#getforcurrentview
109.             PrintManager printMan = PrintManager.GetForCurrentView();
110.             printMan.PrintTaskRequested -= PrintTaskRequested;
111. 
112.             PrintCanvas.Children.Clear();
113.         }
114. 
115.         public async Task ShowPrintUIAsync()
116.         {
117.             // Catch and print out any errors reported
118.             try
119.             {
120.                 await PrintManager.ShowPrintUIAsync();
121.             }
122.             catch (Exception e)
123.             {
124.                 MainPage.Current.NotifyUser("Error printing: " + e.Message + ", hr=" + e.HResult, NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
125.             }
126.         }
127. 
128.         /// <summary>
129.         /// Method that will generate print content for the scenario
130.         /// For scenarios 1-4: it will create the first page from which content will flow
131.         /// Scenario 5 uses a different approach
132.         /// </summary>
133.         /// <param name="page">The page to print</param>
134.         public virtual void PreparePrintContent(Page page)
135.         {
136.             if (firstPage == null)
137.             {
138.                 firstPage = page;
139.                 StackPanel header = (StackPanel)firstPage.FindName("Header");
140.                 header.Visibility = Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible;
141.             }
142. 
143.             // Add the (newly created) page to the print canvas which is part of the visual tree and force it to go
144.             // through layout so that the linked containers correctly distribute the content inside them.
145.             PrintCanvas.Children.Add(firstPage);
146.             PrintCanvas.InvalidateMeasure();
147.             PrintCanvas.UpdateLayout();
148.         }
149. 
150.         /// <summary>
151.         /// This is the event handler for PrintManager.PrintTaskRequested.
152.         /// </summary>
153.         /// <param name="sender">PrintManager</param>
154.         /// <param name="e">PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs </param>
155.         protected virtual void PrintTaskRequested(PrintManager sender, PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs e)
156.         {
157.             PrintTask printTask = null;
158.             printTask = e.Request.CreatePrintTask("C# Printing SDK Sample", sourceRequested =>
159.             {
160.                 // Print Task event handler is invoked when the print job is completed.
161.                 printTask.Completed += async (s, args) =>
162.                 {
163.                     // Notify the user when the print operation fails.
164.                     if (args.Completion == PrintTaskCompletion.Failed)
165.                     {
166.                         // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
167.                         await scenarioPage.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
168.                         {
169.                             MainPage.Current.NotifyUser("Failed to print.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
170.                         });
171.                     }
172.                 };
173. 
174.                 sourceRequested.SetSource(printDocumentSource);
175.             });
176.         }
177. 
178.         /// <summary>
179.         /// This is the event handler for PrintDocument.Paginate. It creates print preview pages for the app.
180.         /// </summary>
181.         /// <param name="sender">PrintDocument</param>
182.         /// <param name="e">Paginate Event Arguments</param>
183.         protected virtual void CreatePrintPreviewPages(object sender, PaginateEventArgs e)
184.         {
185.             lock (printPreviewPages)
186.             {
187.                 // Clear the cache of preview pages
188.                 printPreviewPages.Clear();
189. 
190.                 // Clear the print canvas of preview pages
191.                 PrintCanvas.Children.Clear();
192. 
193.                 // This variable keeps track of the last RichTextBlockOverflow element that was added to a page which will be printed
194.                 RichTextBlockOverflow lastRTBOOnPage;
195. 
196.                 // Get the PrintTaskOptions
197.                 PrintTaskOptions printingOptions = ((PrintTaskOptions)e.PrintTaskOptions);
198. 
199.                 // Get the page description to deterimine how big the page is
200.                 PrintPageDescription pageDescription = printingOptions.GetPageDescription(0);
201. 
202.                 // We know there is at least one page to be printed. passing null as the first parameter to
203.                 // AddOnePrintPreviewPage tells the function to add the first page.
204.                 lastRTBOOnPage = AddOnePrintPreviewPage(null, pageDescription);
205. 
206.                 // We know there are more pages to be added as long as the last RichTextBoxOverflow added to a print preview
207.                 // page has extra content
208.                 while (lastRTBOOnPage.HasOverflowContent && lastRTBOOnPage.Visibility == Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible)
209.                 {
210.                     lastRTBOOnPage = AddOnePrintPreviewPage(lastRTBOOnPage, pageDescription);
211.                 }
212. 
213.                 if (PreviewPagesCreated != null)
214.                 {
215.                     PreviewPagesCreated.Invoke(printPreviewPages, null);
216.                 }
217. 
218.                 PrintDocument printDoc = (PrintDocument)sender;
219. 
220.                 // Report the number of preview pages created
221.                 printDoc.SetPreviewPageCount(printPreviewPages.Count, PreviewPageCountType.Intermediate);
222.             }
223.         }
224. 
225.         /// <summary>
226.         /// This is the event handler for PrintDocument.GetPrintPreviewPage. It provides a specific print preview page,
227.         /// in the form of an UIElement, to an instance of PrintDocument. PrintDocument subsequently converts the UIElement
228.         /// into a page that the Windows print system can deal with.
229.         /// </summary>
230.         /// <param name="sender">PrintDocument</param>
231.         /// <param name="e">Arguments containing the preview requested page</param>
232.         protected virtual void GetPrintPreviewPage(object sender, GetPreviewPageEventArgs e)
233.         {
234.             PrintDocument printDoc = (PrintDocument)sender;
235.             printDoc.SetPreviewPage(e.PageNumber, printPreviewPages[e.PageNumber - 1]);
236.         }
237. 
238.         /// <summary>
239.         /// This is the event handler for PrintDocument.AddPages. It provides all pages to be printed, in the form of
240.         /// UIElements, to an instance of PrintDocument. PrintDocument subsequently converts the UIElements
241.         /// into a pages that the Windows print system can deal with.
242.         /// </summary>
243.         /// <param name="sender">PrintDocument</param>
244.         /// <param name="e">Add page event arguments containing a print task options reference</param>
245.         protected virtual void AddPrintPages(object sender, AddPagesEventArgs e)
246.         {
247.             // Loop over all of the preview pages and add each one to  add each page to be printied
248.             for (int i = 0; i < printPreviewPages.Count; i++)
249.             {
250.                 // We should have all pages ready at this point...
251.                 printDocument.AddPage(printPreviewPages[i]);
252.             }
253.             
254.             PrintDocument printDoc = (PrintDocument)sender;
255.             
256.             // Indicate that all of the print pages have been provided
257.             printDoc.AddPagesComplete();
258.         }
259. 
260.         /// <summary>
261.         /// This function creates and adds one print preview page to the internal cache of print preview
262.         /// pages stored in printPreviewPages.
263.         /// </summary>
264.         /// <param name="lastRTBOAdded">Last RichTextBlockOverflow element added in the current content</param>
265.         /// <param name="printPageDescription">Printer's page description</param>
266.         protected virtual RichTextBlockOverflow AddOnePrintPreviewPage(RichTextBlockOverflow lastRTBOAdded, PrintPageDescription printPageDescription)
267.         {
268.             // XAML element that is used to represent to "printing page"
269.             FrameworkElement page;
270. 
271.             // The link container for text overflowing in this page
272.             RichTextBlockOverflow textLink;
273. 
274.             // Check if this is the first page ( no previous RichTextBlockOverflow)
275.             if (lastRTBOAdded == null)
276.             {
277.                 // If this is the first page add the specific scenario content
278.                 page = firstPage;
279. 
280.                 // Hide footer since we don't know yet if it will be displayed (this might not be the last page) - wait for layout
281.                 StackPanel footer = (StackPanel)page.FindName("Footer");
282.                 footer.Visibility = Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Collapsed;
283.             }
284.             else
285.             {
286.                 // Flow content (text) from previous pages
287.                 page = new ContinuationPage(lastRTBOAdded);
288.             }
289. 
290.             // Set "paper" width
291.             page.Width = printPageDescription.PageSize.Width;
292.             page.Height = printPageDescription.PageSize.Height;
293.             
294.             Grid printableArea = (Grid)page.FindName("PrintableArea");
295. 
296.             // Get the margins size
297.             // If the ImageableRect is smaller than the app provided margins use the ImageableRect
298.             double marginWidth = Math.Max(printPageDescription.PageSize.Width - printPageDescription.ImageableRect.Width, printPageDescription.PageSize.Width * ApplicationContentMarginLeft * 2);
299.             double marginHeight = Math.Max(printPageDescription.PageSize.Height - printPageDescription.ImageableRect.Height, printPageDescription.PageSize.Height * ApplicationContentMarginTop * 2);
300. 
301.             // Set-up "printable area" on the "paper"
302.             printableArea.Width = firstPage.Width - marginWidth;
303.             printableArea.Height = firstPage.Height - marginHeight;
304. 
305.             // Add the (newley created) page to the print canvas which is part of the visual tree and force it to go
306.             // through layout so that the linked containers correctly distribute the content inside them.
307.             PrintCanvas.Children.Add(page);
308.             PrintCanvas.InvalidateMeasure();
309.             PrintCanvas.UpdateLayout();
310. 
311.             // Find the last text container and see if the content is overflowing
312.             textLink = (RichTextBlockOverflow)page.FindName("ContinuationPageLinkedContainer");
313. 
314.             // Check if this is the last page
315.             if (!textLink.HasOverflowContent && textLink.Visibility == Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible)
316.             {
317.                 StackPanel footer = (StackPanel)page.FindName("Footer");
318.                 footer.Visibility = Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible;
319.                 PrintCanvas.UpdateLayout();
320.             }
321. 
322.             // Add the page to the page preview collection
323.             printPreviewPages.Add(page);
324. 
325.             return textLink;
326.         }
327.     }
328. }
329.

20. namespace PrintSample
21. {
22.     class StandardOptionsPrintHelper : PrintHelper
23.     {
24.         public StandardOptionsPrintHelper(Page scenarioPage) : base(scenarioPage) { }
25. 
26.         /// <summary>
27.         /// This is the event handler for PrintManager.PrintTaskRequested.
28.         /// In order to ensure a good user experience, the system requires that the app handle the PrintTaskRequested event within the time specified by PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs.Request.Deadline.
29.         /// Therefore, we use this handler to only create the print task.
30.         /// The print settings customization can be done when the print document source is requested.
31.         /// </summary>
32.         /// <param name="sender">PrintManager</param>
33.         /// <param name="e">PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs</param>
34.         protected override void PrintTaskRequested(PrintManager sender, PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs e)
35.         {
36.             PrintTask printTask = null;
37.             printTask = e.Request.CreatePrintTask("C# Printing SDK Sample", sourceRequestedArgs =>
38.             {
39.                 IList<string> displayedOptions = printTask.Options.DisplayedOptions;
40. 
41.                 // Choose the printer options to be shown.
42.                 // The order in which the options are appended determines the order in which they appear in the UI
43.                 displayedOptions.Clear();
44.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.Copies);
45.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.Orientation);
46.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.MediaSize);
47.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.Collation);
48.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.Duplex);
49. 
50.                 // Preset the default value of the printer option
51.                 printTask.Options.MediaSize = PrintMediaSize.NorthAmericaLegal;
52. 
53.                 // Print Task event handler is invoked when the print job is completed.
54.                 printTask.Completed += async (s, args) =>
55.                 {
56.                     // Notify the user when the print operation fails.
57.                     if (args.Completion == PrintTaskCompletion.Failed)
58.                     {
59.                         // TODO[migrate-004]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
60.                         await scenarioPage.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
61.                         {
62.                             MainPage.Current.NotifyUser("Failed to print.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
63.                         });
64.                     }
65.                 };
66. 
67.                 sourceRequestedArgs.SetSource(printDocumentSource);
68.             });
69.         }
70.     }

26.     [Flags]
27.     internal enum DisplayContent : int
28.     {
29.         /// <summary>
30.         /// Show only text
31.         /// </summary>
32.         Text = 1,
33. 
34.         /// <summary>
35.         /// Show only images
36.         /// </summary>
37.         Images = 2,
38. 
39.         /// <summary>
40.         /// Show a combination of images and text
41.         /// </summary>
42.         TextAndImages = 3
43.     }
44. 
45.     class CustomOptionsPrintHelper : PrintHelper
46.     {
47.         /// <summary>
48.         /// A flag that determines if text & images are to be shown
49.         /// </summary>
50.         internal DisplayContent imageText = DisplayContent.TextAndImages;
51. 
52.         /// <summary>
53.         /// Helper getter for text showing
54.         /// </summary>
55.         private bool ShowText
56.         {
57.             get { return (imageText & DisplayContent.Text) == DisplayContent.Text; }
58.         }
59. 
60.         /// <summary>
61.         /// Helper getter for image showing
62.         /// </summary>
63.         private bool ShowImage
64.         {
65.             get { return (imageText & DisplayContent.Images) == DisplayContent.Images; }
66.         }
67. 
68.         private bool showHeader = true;
69. 
70.         Task<IRandomAccessStreamWithContentType> wideMarginsIconTask;
71.         Task<IRandomAccessStreamWithContentType> moderateMarginsIconTask;
72.         Task<IRandomAccessStreamWithContentType> narrowMarginsIconTask;
73. 
74.         public CustomOptionsPrintHelper(Page scenarioPage) : base(scenarioPage)
75.         {
76.             // Start these tasks early because we know we're going to need the
77.             // streams in PrintTaskRequested.
78.             RandomAccessStreamReference wideMarginsIconReference = RandomAccessStreamReference.CreateFromUri(new Uri("ms-appx:///Assets/wideMargins.svg"));
79.             wideMarginsIconTask = wideMarginsIconReference.OpenReadAsync().AsTask();
80. 
81.             RandomAccessStreamReference moderateMarginsIconReference = RandomAccessStreamReference.CreateFromUri(new Uri("ms-appx:///Assets/moderateMargins.svg"));
82.             moderateMarginsIconTask = moderateMarginsIconReference.OpenReadAsync().AsTask();
83. 
84.             RandomAccessStreamReference narrowMarginsIconReference = RandomAccessStreamReference.CreateFromUri(new Uri("ms-appx:///Assets/narrowMargins.svg"));
85.             narrowMarginsIconTask = narrowMarginsIconReference.OpenReadAsync().AsTask();
86.         }
87. 
88.         /// <summary>
89.         /// This is the event handler for PrintManager.PrintTaskRequested.
90.         /// In order to ensure a good user experience, the system requires that the app handle the PrintTaskRequested event within the time specified by PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs.Request.Deadline.
91.         /// Therefore, we use this handler to only create the print task.
92.         /// The print settings customization can be done when the print document source is requested.
93.         /// </summary>
94.         /// <param name="sender">PrintManager</param>
95.         /// <param name="e">PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs</param>
96.         protected override void PrintTaskRequested(PrintManager sender, PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs e)
97.         {
98.             PrintTask printTask = null;
99.             printTask = e.Request.CreatePrintTask("C# Printing SDK Sample", async sourceRequestedArgs =>
100.             {
101.                 var deferral = sourceRequestedArgs.GetDeferral();
102.                 PrintTaskOptionDetails printDetailedOptions = PrintTaskOptionDetails.GetFromPrintTaskOptions(printTask.Options);
103.                 IList<string> displayedOptions = printDetailedOptions.DisplayedOptions;
104. 
105.                 // Choose the printer options to be shown.
106.                 // The order in which the options are appended determines the order in which they appear in the UI
107.                 displayedOptions.Clear();
108. 
109.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.Copies);
110.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.Orientation);
111.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.ColorMode);
112. 
113.                 // Create a new list option
114.                 PrintCustomItemListOptionDetails pageFormat = printDetailedOptions.CreateItemListOption("PageContent", "Pictures");
115.                 pageFormat.AddItem("PicturesText", "Pictures and text");
116.                 pageFormat.AddItem("PicturesOnly", "Pictures only");
117.                 pageFormat.AddItem("TextOnly", "Text only");
118. 
119.                 // Add the custom option to the option list
120.                 displayedOptions.Add("PageContent");
121.                 
122.                 // Create a new toggle option "Show header". 
123.                 PrintCustomToggleOptionDetails header = printDetailedOptions.CreateToggleOption("Header", "Show header");
124. 
125.                 // App tells the user some more information about what the feature means.
126.                 header.Description = "Display a header on the first page";
127. 
128.                 // Set the default value
129.                 header.TrySetValue(showHeader);
130.                 
131.                 // Add the custom option to the option list
132.                 displayedOptions.Add("Header");
133.                 
134.                 // Create a new list option
135.                 PrintCustomItemListOptionDetails margins = printDetailedOptions.CreateItemListOption("Margins", "Margins");
136.                 margins.AddItem("WideMargins", "Wide", "Each margin is 20% of the paper size", await wideMarginsIconTask);
137.                 margins.AddItem("ModerateMargins", "Moderate", "Each margin is 10% of the paper size", await moderateMarginsIconTask);
138.                 margins.AddItem("NarrowMargins", "Narrow", "Each margin is 5% of the paper size", await narrowMarginsIconTask);
139.                 
140.                 // The default is ModerateMargins
141.                 ApplicationContentMarginTop = 0.1;
142.                 ApplicationContentMarginLeft = 0.1;
143.                 margins.TrySetValue("ModerateMargins");
144. 
145.                 // App tells the user some more information about what the feature means.
146.                 margins.Description = "The space between the content of your document and the edge of the paper";
147. 
148.                 // Add the custom option to the option list
149.                 displayedOptions.Add("Margins");
150.                 
151.                 printDetailedOptions.OptionChanged += printDetailedOptions_OptionChanged;
152. 
153.                 // Print Task event handler is invoked when the print job is completed.
154.                 printTask.Completed += (s, args) =>
155.                 {
156.                     // Notify the user when the print operation fails.
157.                     if (args.Completion == PrintTaskCompletion.Failed)
158.                     {
159.                         MainPage.Current.NotifyUser("Failed to print.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
160.                     }
161.                 };
162. 
163.                 sourceRequestedArgs.SetSource(printDocumentSource);
164. 
165.                 deferral.Complete();
166.             });
167.         }
168. 
169.         /// <summary>
170.         /// This is the event handler for whenever the user makes changes to the options. 
171.         /// In this case, the options of interest are PageContent, Margins and Header.
172.         /// </summary>
173.         /// <param name="sender">PrintTaskOptionDetails</param>
174.         /// <param name="args">PrintTaskOptionChangedEventArgs</param>
175.         async void printDetailedOptions_OptionChanged(PrintTaskOptionDetails sender, PrintTaskOptionChangedEventArgs args)
176.         {
177.             bool invalidatePreview = false;
178.             
179.             string optionId = args.OptionId as string;
180.             if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionId))
181.             {
182.                 return;
183.             }
184. 
185.             if (optionId == "PageContent")
186.             {
187.                 invalidatePreview = true;
188.             }
189. 
190.             if (optionId == "Margins")
191.             {
192.                 PrintCustomItemListOptionDetails marginsOption = (PrintCustomItemListOptionDetails)sender.Options["Margins"];
193.                 string marginsValue = marginsOption.Value.ToString();
194.                 
195.                 switch (marginsValue)
196.                 {
197.                     case "WideMargins":
198.                         ApplicationContentMarginTop = 0.2;
199.                         ApplicationContentMarginLeft = 0.2; 
200.                         break;
201.                     case "ModerateMargins":
202.                         ApplicationContentMarginTop = 0.1;
203.                         ApplicationContentMarginLeft = 0.1;
204.                         break;
205.                     case "NarrowMargins":
206.                         ApplicationContentMarginTop = 0.05;
207.                         ApplicationContentMarginLeft = 0.05;
208.                         break;
209.                 }
210. 
211.                 if (marginsValue == "NarrowMargins")
212.                 {
213.                     marginsOption.WarningText = "Narrow margins may not be supported by some printers";
214.                 }
215.                 else
216.                 {
217.                     marginsOption.WarningText = "";
218.                 }
219. 
220.                 invalidatePreview = true;
221.             }
222. 
223.             if (optionId == "Header")
224.             {
225.                 PrintCustomToggleOptionDetails headerOption = (PrintCustomToggleOptionDetails)sender.Options["Header"];
226.                 showHeader = (bool)headerOption.Value;
227.                 invalidatePreview = true;
228.             }
229. 
230.             if (invalidatePreview)
231.             {
232.                 // TODO[migrate-005]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
233.                 await scenarioPage.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
234.                 {
235.                     printDocument.InvalidatePreview();
236.                 });
237.             }
238.         }
239. 
240.         #region PrintPreview
241. 
242.         protected override RichTextBlockOverflow AddOnePrintPreviewPage(RichTextBlockOverflow lastRTBOAdded, PrintPageDescription printPageDescription)
243.         {
244.             // Check if we need to hide/show text & images for this scenario
245.             // Since all is rulled by the first page (page flow), here is where we must start
246.             if (lastRTBOAdded == null)
247.             {
248.                 // Get a refference to page objects
249.                 Grid pageContent = (Grid)firstPage.FindName("PrintableArea");
250.                 Image scenarioImage = (Image)firstPage.FindName("ScenarioImage");
251.                 RichTextBlock mainText = (RichTextBlock)firstPage.FindName("TextContent");
252.                 RichTextBlockOverflow firstLink = (RichTextBlockOverflow)firstPage.FindName("FirstLinkedContainer");
253.                 RichTextBlockOverflow continuationLink = (RichTextBlockOverflow)firstPage.FindName("ContinuationPageLinkedContainer");
254. 
255.                 // Hide(collapse) and move elements in different grid cells depending by the viewable content(only text, only pictures)
256. 
257.                 scenarioImage.Visibility = ShowImage ? Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible : Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Collapsed;
258.                 firstLink.SetValue(Grid.ColumnSpanProperty, ShowImage ? 1 : 2);
259. 
260.                 scenarioImage.SetValue(Grid.RowProperty, ShowText ? 2 : 1);
261.                 scenarioImage.SetValue(Grid.ColumnProperty, ShowText ? 1 : 0);
262. 
263.                 pageContent.ColumnDefinitions[0].Width = new GridLength(ShowText ? 6 : 4, GridUnitType.Star);
264.                 pageContent.ColumnDefinitions[1].Width = new GridLength(ShowText ? 4 : 6, GridUnitType.Star);
265. 
266.                 // Break the text flow if the app is not printing text in order not to spawn pages with blank content
267.                 mainText.Visibility = ShowText ? Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible : Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Collapsed;
268.                 continuationLink.Visibility = ShowText ? Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible : Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Collapsed;
269. 
270.                 // Hide header if appropriate
271.                 StackPanel header = (StackPanel)firstPage.FindName("Header");
272.                 header.Visibility = showHeader ? Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible : Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Collapsed;
273.             }
274. 
275.             //Continue with the rest of the base printing layout processing (paper size, printable page size)
276.             return base.AddOnePrintPreviewPage(lastRTBOAdded, printPageDescription);
277.         }
278. 
279.         protected override void CreatePrintPreviewPages(object sender, PaginateEventArgs e)
280.         {
281.             // Get PageContent property
282.             PrintTaskOptionDetails printDetailedOptions = PrintTaskOptionDetails.GetFromPrintTaskOptions(e.PrintTaskOptions);
283.             string pageContent = (printDetailedOptions.Options["PageContent"].Value as string).ToLowerInvariant();
284. 
285.             // Set the text & image display flag
286.             imageText = (DisplayContent)((Convert.ToInt32(pageContent.Contains("pictures")) << 1) | Convert.ToInt32(pageContent.Contains("text")));
287. 
288.             base.CreatePrintPreviewPages(sender, e);
289.         }
290. 
291.         #endregion
292. 
293.     }

33.     class PageRangePrintHelper : PrintHelper
34.     {
35.         public PageRangePrintHelper(Page scenarioPage) : base(scenarioPage)
36.         {
37.             // Use moderate margins to allow the content to span multiple pages
38.             ApplicationContentMarginTop = 0.1;
39.             ApplicationContentMarginLeft = 0.1;
40.         }
41. 
42.         /// <summary>
43.         /// This is the event handler for PrintManager.PrintTaskRequested.
44.         /// In order to ensure a good user experience, the system requires that the app handle the PrintTaskRequested event within the time specified by PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs.Request.Deadline.
45.         /// Therefore, we use this handler to only create the print task.
46.         /// The print settings customization can be done when the print document source is requested.
47.         /// </summary>
48.         /// <param name="sender">PrintManager</param>
49.         /// <param name="e">PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs</param>
50.         protected override void PrintTaskRequested(PrintManager sender, PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs e)
51.         {
52.             PrintTask printTask = null;
53.             printTask = e.Request.CreatePrintTask("C# Printing SDK Sample", sourceRequestedArgs =>
54.             {
55.                 PrintTaskOptionDetails printDetailedOptions = PrintTaskOptionDetails.GetFromPrintTaskOptions(printTask.Options);
56.                 IList<string> displayedOptions = printDetailedOptions.DisplayedOptions;
57. 
58.                 // Choose the printer options to be shown.
59.                 // The order in which the options are appended determines the order in which they appear in the UI
60.                 displayedOptions.Clear();
61. 
62.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.Copies);
63.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.CustomPageRanges);
64.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.Orientation);
65.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.MediaSize);
66.                 displayedOptions.Add(Windows.Graphics.Printing.StandardPrintTaskOptions.ColorMode);
67. 
68.                 printTask.Options.PageRangeOptions.AllowCurrentPage = true;
69.                 printTask.Options.PageRangeOptions.AllowAllPages = true;
70.                 printTask.Options.PageRangeOptions.AllowCustomSetOfPages = true;
71. 
72.                 // Register the handler for the option change event
73.                 printDetailedOptions.OptionChanged += printDetailedOptions_OptionChanged;
74. 
75.                 // Register the handler for the PrintTask.Completed event.
76.                 // Print Task event handler is invoked when the print job is completed.
77.                 printTask.Completed += async (s, args) =>
78.                 {
79.                     // Notify the user when the print operation fails.
80.                     if (args.Completion == PrintTaskCompletion.Failed)
81.                     {
82.                         // TODO[migrate-006]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
83.                         await scenarioPage.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
84.                         {
85.                             MainPage.Current.NotifyUser("Failed to print.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
86.                         });
87.                     }
88.                 };
89. 
90.                 sourceRequestedArgs.SetSource(printDocumentSource);
91.             });
92.         }
93. 
94.         /// <summary>
95.         /// This is the event handler for PrintDocument.Paginate. It creates print preview pages for the app.
96.         /// </summary>
97.         /// <param name="sender">PrintDocument</param>
98.         /// <param name="e">Paginate Event Arguments</param>
99.         protected override void CreatePrintPreviewPages(object sender, PaginateEventArgs e)
100.         {
101.             base.CreatePrintPreviewPages(sender, e);
102. 
103.             PrintTaskOptionDetails printDetailedOptions = PrintTaskOptionDetails.GetFromPrintTaskOptions(e.PrintTaskOptions);
104.             PrintPageRangeOptionDetails pageRangeOption = (PrintPageRangeOptionDetails)printDetailedOptions.Options[StandardPrintTaskOptions.CustomPageRanges];
105. 
106.             // The number of pages may have been changed, so validate the Page Ranges again
107.             ValidatePageRangeOption(pageRangeOption);
108.         }
109. 
110.         /// <summary>
111.         /// This is the event handler for PrintDocument.AddPages. It provides all pages to be printed, in the form of
112.         /// UIElements, to an instance of PrintDocument. PrintDocument subsequently converts the UIElements
113.         /// into a pages that the Windows print system can deal with.
114.         /// </summary>
115.         /// <param name="sender">PrintDocument</param>
116.         /// <param name="e">Add page event arguments containing a print task options reference</param>
117.         protected override void AddPrintPages(object sender, AddPagesEventArgs e)
118.         {
119.             IList<PrintPageRange> customPageRanges = e.PrintTaskOptions.CustomPageRanges;
120. 
121.             // An empty CustomPageRanges means "All Pages"
122.             if (customPageRanges.Count == 0)
123.             {
124.                 // Loop over all of the preview pages and add each one to be printed
125.                 foreach (UIElement previewPage in printPreviewPages)
126.                 {
127.                     printDocument.AddPage(previewPage);
128.                 }
129.             }
130.             else
131.             {
132.                 // Print only the pages chosen by the user.
133.                 // 
134.                 // The "Current page" option is a special case of "Custom set of pages".
135.                 // In case the user selects the "Current page" option, the PrintDialog
136.                 // will turn that into a CustomPageRanges containing the page that the user was looking at.
137.                 // If the user typed in an indefinite range such as "6-", the LastPageNumber value
138.                 // will be whatever this sample app last passed into the PrintDocument.SetPreviewPageCount API.
139.                 foreach (PrintPageRange pageRange in customPageRanges)
140.                 {
141.                     // The user may type in a page number that is not present in the document.
142.                     // In this case, we just ignore those pages, hence the checks
143.                     // (pageRange.FirstPageNumber <= printPreviewPages.Count) and (i <= printPreviewPages.Count).
144.                     //
145.                     // If the user types the same page multiple times, it will be printed multiple times
146.                     // (e.g 3-4;1;1 will print pages 3 and 4 followed by two copies of page 1)
147.                     if (pageRange.FirstPageNumber <= printPreviewPages.Count)
148.                     {
149.                         for (int i = pageRange.FirstPageNumber; (i <= pageRange.LastPageNumber) && (i <= printPreviewPages.Count); i++)
150.                         {
151.                             // Subtract 1 because page numbers are 1-based, but our list is 0-based.
152.                             printDocument.AddPage(printPreviewPages[i - 1]);
153.                         }
154.                     }
155.                 }
156.             }
157. 
158.             PrintDocument printDoc = (PrintDocument)sender;
159. 
160.             // Indicate that all of the print pages have been provided
161.             printDoc.AddPagesComplete();
162.         }
163. 
164.         /// <summary>
165.         /// Option change event handler
166.         /// </summary>
167.         /// <param name="sender">PrintTaskOptionsDetails object</param>
168.         /// <param name="args">the event arguments containing the changed option id</param>
169.         void printDetailedOptions_OptionChanged(PrintTaskOptionDetails sender, PrintTaskOptionChangedEventArgs args)
170.         {
171.             string optionId = args.OptionId as string;
172.             if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionId))
173.             {
174.                 return;
175.             }
176. 
177.             // Handle change in Page Range Option
178.             if (optionId == StandardPrintTaskOptions.CustomPageRanges)
179.             {
180.                 PrintPageRangeOptionDetails pageRangeOption = (PrintPageRangeOptionDetails)sender.Options[optionId];
181.                 ValidatePageRangeOption(pageRangeOption);
182.             }
183.         }
184. 
185.         /// <summary>
186.         /// Deals with validating that the Page Ranges only contain pages that are present in the document.
187.         /// 
188.         /// This is not necessary and some apps don't do this validation. Instead, they just ignore the pages
189.         /// that are not present in the document and simply don't print them.
190.         /// </summary>
191.         /// <param name="pageRangeOption">The PrintPageRangeOptionDetails option</param>
192.         void ValidatePageRangeOption(PrintPageRangeOptionDetails pageRangeOption)
193.         {
194.             IList<PrintPageRange> pageRanges = ((IList<PrintPageRange>)pageRangeOption.Value).ToImmutableList();
195. 
196.             pageRangeOption.WarningText = "";
197.             pageRangeOption.ErrorText = "";
198. 
199.             // An empty list means AllPages, in which case we don't need to check the ranges
200.             if (pageRanges.Count > 0)
201.             {
202.                 lock (printPreviewPages)
203.                 {
204.                     // Verify that the page ranges contain at least one printable page
205.                     bool containsAtLeastOnePrintablePage = false;
206.                     foreach (PrintPageRange pageRange in pageRanges)
207.                     {
208.                         if ((pageRange.FirstPageNumber <= printPreviewPages.Count) || (pageRange.LastPageNumber <= printPreviewPages.Count))
209.                         {
210.                             containsAtLeastOnePrintablePage = true;
211.                             break;
212.                         }
213.                     }
214. 
215.                     if (containsAtLeastOnePrintablePage)
216.                     {
217.                         // Warn the user in case one of the page ranges contains pages that will not printed. That way, s/he
218.                         // can fix the page numbers in case they were mistyped.
219.                         foreach (PrintPageRange pageRange in pageRanges)
220.                         {
221.                             if ((pageRange.FirstPageNumber > printPreviewPages.Count) || (pageRange.LastPageNumber > printPreviewPages.Count))
222.                             {
223.                                 pageRangeOption.WarningText = "One of the ranges contains pages that are not present in the document";
224.                                 break;
225.                             }
226.                         }
227.                     }
228.                     else
229.                     {
230.                         pageRangeOption.ErrorText = "Those pages are not present in the document";
231.                     }
232.                 }
233.             }
234.         }
235.     }
236.

12. using SDKTemplate;
13. using System;
14. using System.Collections.Generic;
15. using Windows.Foundation.Metadata;
16. using Windows.Graphics.Printing;
17. using Windows.UI.Core;

20. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
21. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Printing;

23. 
24. namespace PrintSample
25. {
26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// This class customizes the standard PrintHelper in order to disable print preview.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     /// <remarks>
30.     /// The PrintTaskRequestedMethod sets printTask.IsPreviewEnabled to false in order
31.     /// to disable print preview.
32.     /// Since print preview is disabled, the Paginate event will not be raised.
33.     /// Instead, the pages to be printed are generated in response to the AddPages event.
34.     /// </remarks>
35.     class DisablePreviewPrintHelper : PrintHelper
36.     {
37.         /// <summary>
38.         /// A list of UIElements used to store the print pages.  This gives easy access
39.         /// to any desired print page.
40.         /// </summary>
41.         internal List<UIElement> printPages;
42. 
43.         public DisablePreviewPrintHelper(Page scenarioPage) : base(scenarioPage) {
44.             printPages = new List<UIElement>();
45.         }
46. 
47.         /// <summary>
48.         /// This is the event handler for PrintManager.PrintTaskRequested.
49.         /// In order to ensure a good user experience, the system requires that the app handle the PrintTaskRequested event within the time specified by PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs.Request.Deadline.
50.         /// Therefore, we use this handler to only create the print task.
51.         /// The print settings customization can be done when the print document source is requested.
52.         /// </summary>
53.         /// <param name="sender">PrintManager</param>
54.         /// <param name="e">PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs</param>
55.         protected override void PrintTaskRequested(PrintManager sender, PrintTaskRequestedEventArgs e)
56.         {
57.             PrintTask printTask = null;
58.             printTask = e.Request.CreatePrintTask("C# Printing SDK Sample", sourceRequestedArgs =>
59.             {
60.                 // Print Task event handler is invoked when the print job is completed.
61.                 printTask.Completed += async (s, args) =>
62.                 {
63.                     // Notify the user when the print operation fails.
64.                     if (args.Completion == PrintTaskCompletion.Failed)
65.                     {
66.                         // TODO[migrate-009]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
67.                         await scenarioPage.Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
68.                         {
69.                             MainPage.Current.NotifyUser("Failed to print.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
70.                         });
71.                     }
72.                 };
73. 
74.                 sourceRequestedArgs.SetSource(printDocumentSource);
75.             });
76. 
77.             // Choose not to show the preview by setting the property on PrintTask
78.             printTask.IsPreviewEnabled = false;
79.         }
80. 
81.         /// <summary>
82.         /// This is the event handler for PrintDocument.AddPages. It provides all pages to be printed, in the form of
83.         /// UIElements, to an instance of PrintDocument. PrintDocument subsequently converts the UIElements
84.         /// into a pages that the Windows print system can deal with.
85.         /// </summary>
86.         /// <param name="sender">PrintDocument</param>
87.         /// <param name="e">Add page event arguments containing a print task options reference</param>
88.         protected override void AddPrintPages(object sender, AddPagesEventArgs e)
89.         {
90.             // Clear the cache of print pages
91.             printPages.Clear();
92. 
93.             // Clear the print canvas of print pages
94.             PrintCanvas.Children.Clear();
95. 
96.             // This variable keeps track of the last RichTextBlockOverflow element that was added to a page which will be printed
97.             RichTextBlockOverflow lastRTBOOnPage;
98. 
99.             // Get the PrintTaskOptions
100.             PrintTaskOptions printingOptions = ((PrintTaskOptions)e.PrintTaskOptions);
101. 
102.             // Get the page description to deterimine how big the page is
103.             PrintPageDescription pageDescription = printingOptions.GetPageDescription(0);
104. 
105.             // We know there is at least one page to be printed. passing null as the first parameter to
106.             // AddOnePrintPage tells the function to add the first page.
107.             lastRTBOOnPage = AddOnePrintPage(null, pageDescription);
108. 
109.             // We know there are more pages to be added as long as the last RichTextBoxOverflow added to a print
110.             // page has extra content
111.             while (lastRTBOOnPage.HasOverflowContent && (lastRTBOOnPage.Visibility == Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible))
112.             {
113.                 lastRTBOOnPage = AddOnePrintPage(lastRTBOOnPage, pageDescription);
114.             }
115. 
116.             // Loop over all of the pages and add each one to add each page to be printed
117.             for (int i = 0; i < printPages.Count; i++)
118.             {
119.                 // We should have all pages ready at this point...
120.                 printDocument.AddPage(printPages[i]);
121.             }
122. 
123.             PrintDocument printDoc = (PrintDocument)sender;
124. 
125.             // Indicate that all of the print pages have been provided
126.             printDoc.AddPagesComplete();
127.         }
128. 
129.         /// <summary>
130.         /// This function creates and adds one print page to the internal cache of print pages
131.         /// pages stored in printPages.
132.         /// </summary>
133.         /// <param name="lastRTBOAdded">Last RichTextBlockOverflow element added in the current content</param>
134.         /// <param name="printPageDescription">Printer's page description</param>
135.         protected RichTextBlockOverflow AddOnePrintPage(RichTextBlockOverflow lastRTBOAdded, PrintPageDescription printPageDescription)
136.         {
137.             // XAML element that is used to represent to "printing page"
138.             FrameworkElement page;
139. 
140.             // The link container for text overflowing in this page
141.             RichTextBlockOverflow textLink;
142. 
143.             // Check if this is the first page ( no previous RichTextBlockOverflow)
144.             if (lastRTBOAdded == null)
145.             {
146.                 // If this is the first page add the specific scenario content
147.                 page = firstPage;
148. 
149.                 // Hide footer since we don't know yet if it will be displayed (this might not be the last page) - wait for layout
150.                 StackPanel footer = (StackPanel)page.FindName("Footer");
151.                 footer.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
152.             }
153.             else
154.             {
155.                 // Flow content (text) from previous pages
156.                 page = new ContinuationPage(lastRTBOAdded);
157.             }
158. 
159.             // Set "paper" width
160.             page.Width = printPageDescription.PageSize.Width;
161.             page.Height = printPageDescription.PageSize.Height;
162. 
163.             Grid printableArea = (Grid)page.FindName("PrintableArea");
164. 
165.             // Get the margins size
166.             // If the ImageableRect is smaller than the app provided margins use the ImageableRect
167.             double marginWidth = Math.Max(printPageDescription.PageSize.Width - printPageDescription.ImageableRect.Width, printPageDescription.PageSize.Width * ApplicationContentMarginLeft * 2);
168.             double marginHeight = Math.Max(printPageDescription.PageSize.Height - printPageDescription.ImageableRect.Height, printPageDescription.PageSize.Height * ApplicationContentMarginTop * 2);
169. 
170.             // Set-up "printable area" on the "paper"
171.             printableArea.Width = firstPage.Width - marginWidth;
172.             printableArea.Height = firstPage.Height - marginHeight;
173. 
174.             // Add the (newly created) page to the print canvas which is part of the visual tree and force it to go
175.             // through layout so that the linked containers correctly distribute the content inside them.
176.             PrintCanvas.Children.Add(page);
177.             PrintCanvas.InvalidateMeasure();
178.             PrintCanvas.UpdateLayout();
179. 
180.             // Find the last text container and see if the content is overflowing
181.             textLink = (RichTextBlockOverflow)page.FindName("ContinuationPageLinkedContainer");
182. 
183.             // Check if this is the last page
184.             if ((!textLink.HasOverflowContent) && (textLink.Visibility == Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Visibility.Visible))
185.             {
186.                 StackPanel footer = (StackPanel)page.FindName("Footer");
187.                 footer.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
188.                 PrintCanvas.UpdateLayout();
189.             }
190. 
191.             // Add the page to the print page collection
192.             printPages.Add(page);
193. 
194.             return textLink;
195.         }
196.     }

32.     /// <summary>
33.     /// Photo size options

47.     {
48.         ShrinkToFit,
49.         Crop
50.     }
51. 
52.     /// <summary>
53.     /// Printable page description
54.     /// </summary>
55.     public class PageDescription : IEquatable<PageDescription>
56.     {
57.         public Size Margin;
58.         public Size PageSize;
59.         public Size ViewablePageSize;
60.         public Size PictureViewSize;
61.         public bool IsContentCropped;
62. 
63.         public bool Equals(PageDescription other)
64.         {
65.             // Detect if PageSize changed
66.             bool equal = (Math.Abs(PageSize.Width - other.PageSize.Width) < double.Epsilon) &&
67.                 (Math.Abs(PageSize.Height - other.PageSize.Height) < double.Epsilon);
68. 
69.             // Detect if ViewablePageSize changed
70.             if (equal)
71.             {
72.                 equal = (Math.Abs(ViewablePageSize.Width - other.ViewablePageSize.Width) < double.Epsilon) &&
73.                     (Math.Abs(ViewablePageSize.Height - other.ViewablePageSize.Height) < double.Epsilon);
74.             }
75. 
76.             // Detect if PictureViewSize changed
77.             if (equal)
78.             {
79.                 equal = (Math.Abs(PictureViewSize.Width - other.PictureViewSize.Width) < double.Epsilon) &&
80.                     (Math.Abs(PictureViewSize.Height - other.PictureViewSize.Height) < double.Epsilon);
81.             }
82. 
83.             // Detect if cropping changed
84.             if (equal)
85.             {
86.                 equal = IsContentCropped == other.IsContentCropped;
87.             }
88. 
89.             return equal;
90.         }
91.     }

<a id="getforcurrentview"></a>
## GetForCurrentView() ReplacementsNone of the `GetForCurrentView()` patterns work in WinUI 3 desktop — there is no implicit per-view singleton.| UWP API | WinUI 3 Replacement |
|---------|---------------------|
| `ApplicationView.GetForCurrentView()` | `AppWindow.GetFromWindowId(windowId)` |
| `UIViewSettings.GetForCurrentView()` | `AppWindow` properties (size, presenter) |
| `DisplayInformation.GetForCurrentView()` | `XamlRoot.RasterizationScale` or Win32 `GetDpiForWindow` |
| `CoreApplication.GetCurrentView()` | Track windows manually in `App` |
| `SystemNavigationManager.GetForCurrentView()` | Wire back handling in `NavigationView` / `BackRequested` directly |### `DisplayInformation` is view-bound — not just `GetForCurrentView()``DisplayInformation` is CoreWindow/`ApplicationView`-bound. **Every** member — the
`GetForCurrentView()` instance path **and the static members** — compiles fine but
**throws at runtime** in a WinUI 3 desktop app. The `GetForCurrentView()` row above only
covers the DPI use case; the orientation members are the ones that silently blank
orientation/rotation samples:| UWP `DisplayInformation` member | WinUI 3 desktop replacement |
|---------------------------------|-----------------------------|
| `.AutoRotationPreferences` (static get/set) | **No desktop equivalent** — orientation lock is not a desktop windowing concept. Drop the get/set (or store the intent yourself). |
| `.CurrentOrientation` / `.NativeOrientation` | Read via Win32 `EnumDisplaySettings` (`DEVMODE.dmDisplayOrientation` + `dmPelsWidth/Height`). |
| `.OrientationChanged` (via `GetForCurrentView()`) | Subscribe to `AppWindow.Changed` and re-query the Win32 values. |These are **runtime traps**: the build is clean and the analyzer is silent, so treat any
`DisplayInformation` reference as adaptable and resolve it before shipping.

<a id="threading"></a>
## Threading: CoreDispatcher → DispatcherQueueUWP (replace this):```csharp
await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => StatusText.Text = "Done");
```WinUI 3:```csharp
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => StatusText.Text = "Done");
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(DispatcherQueuePriority.High, () => ProgressBar.Value = 100);
```Cache the queue off the UI thread via `DispatcherQueue.GetForCurrentThread()`. UWP's ASTA reentrancy protection is gone — watch for reentrancy in async code that pumps messages. See the official [threading guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/threading).

232.             // For this scenario we are interested only when the 2 custom options change (photoSize & scaling) in order to trigger a preview refresh.

300.         /// <param name="e">The pagination event arguments containing the print options.</param>
301.         protected override void CreatePrintPreviewPages(object sender, Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Printing.PaginateEventArgs e)
302.         {
303. 
304.             PrintDocument printDoc = (PrintDocument)sender;
305. 
306.             // A new "session" starts with each paginate event.
307.             Interlocked.Increment(ref requestCount);
308. 
309.             PageDescription pageDescription = new PageDescription();
310. 
311.             // Get printer's page description.
312.             PrintTaskOptionDetails printDetailedOptions = PrintTaskOptionDetails.GetFromPrintTaskOptions(e.PrintTaskOptions);
313.             PrintPageDescription printPageDescription = e.PrintTaskOptions.GetPageDescription(0);
314. 
315.             // Reset the error state
316.             printDetailedOptions.Options["photoSize"].ErrorText = string.Empty;
317. 
318.             // Compute the printing page description (page size & center printable area)
319.             pageDescription.PageSize = printPageDescription.PageSize;
320. 
321.             pageDescription.Margin.Width = Math.Max(
322.                 printPageDescription.ImageableRect.Left,
323.                 printPageDescription.ImageableRect.Right - printPageDescription.PageSize.Width);
324. 
325.             pageDescription.Margin.Height = Math.Max(
326.                 printPageDescription.ImageableRect.Top,
327.                 printPageDescription.ImageableRect.Bottom - printPageDescription.PageSize.Height);
328. 
329.             pageDescription.ViewablePageSize.Width = printPageDescription.PageSize.Width - pageDescription.Margin.Width * 2;
330.             pageDescription.ViewablePageSize.Height = printPageDescription.PageSize.Height - pageDescription.Margin.Height * 2;
331. 
332.             // Compute print photo area.
333.             switch (photoSize)
334.             {
335.                 case PhotoSize.Size4x6:
336.                     pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Width = 4 * DPI96;
337.                     pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Height = 6 * DPI96;
338.                     break;
339.                 case PhotoSize.Size5x7:
340.                     pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Width = 5 * DPI96;
341.                     pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Height = 7 * DPI96;
342.                     break;
343.                 case PhotoSize.Size8x10:
344.                     pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Width = 8 * DPI96;
345.                     pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Height = 10 * DPI96;
346.                     break;
347.                 case PhotoSize.SizeFullPage:
348.                     pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Width = pageDescription.ViewablePageSize.Width;
349.                     pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Height = pageDescription.ViewablePageSize.Height;
350.                     break;
351.             }
352. 
353.             // Try to maximize photo-size based on it's aspect-ratio
354.             if ((pageDescription.ViewablePageSize.Width > pageDescription.ViewablePageSize.Height) && (photoSize != PhotoSize.SizeFullPage))
355.             {
356.                 var swap = pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Width;
357.                 pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Width = pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Height;
358.                 pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Height = swap;
359.             }
360. 
361.             pageDescription.IsContentCropped = photoScale == Scaling.Crop;
362. 
363.             // Recreate content only when :
364.             // - there is no current page description
365.             // - the current page description doesn't match the new one
366.             if (currentPageDescription == null || !currentPageDescription.Equals(pageDescription))
367.             {
368.                 ClearPageCollection();
369. 
370.                 if (pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Width > pageDescription.ViewablePageSize.Width ||
371.                     pageDescription.PictureViewSize.Height > pageDescription.ViewablePageSize.Height)
372.                 {
373.                     printDetailedOptions.Options["photoSize"].ErrorText = "Photo doesn’t fit on the selected paper";
374. 
375.                     // Inform preview that it has only 1 page to show.
376.                     printDoc.SetPreviewPageCount(1, PreviewPageCountType.Intermediate);
377. 
378.                     // Add a custom "preview" unavailable page
379.                     lock (printSync)
380.                     {
381.                         pageCollection[0] = new PreviewUnavailable(pageDescription.PageSize, pageDescription.ViewablePageSize);
382.                     }
383.                 }
384.                 else
385.                 {
386.                     // Inform preview that is has #NumberOfPhotos pages to show.
387.                     printDoc.SetPreviewPageCount(NumberOfPhotos, PreviewPageCountType.Intermediate);
388.                 }
389. 
390.                 currentPageDescription = pageDescription;
391.             }
392.         }
393. 
394.         /// <summary>
395.         /// This is the event handler for PrintDocument.GetPrintPreviewPage. It provides a specific print page preview,
396.         /// in the form of an UIElement, to an instance of PrintDocument.
397.         /// PrintDocument subsequently converts the UIElement into a page that the Windows print system can deal with.
398.         /// </summary>
399.         /// <param name="sender">The print documet.</param>
400.         /// <param name="e">Arguments containing the requested page preview.</param>
401.         protected async override void GetPrintPreviewPage(object sender, GetPreviewPageEventArgs e)
402.         {
403.             // Store a local copy of the request count to use later to determine if the computed page is out of date.
404.             // If the page preview is unavailable an async operation will generate the content.
405.             // When the operation completes there is a chance that a pagination request was already made therefore making this page obsolete.
406.             // If the page is obsolete throw away the result (don't call SetPreviewPage) since a new GetPrintPreviewPage will server that request.
407.             long requestNumber = 0;
408.             Interlocked.Exchange(ref requestNumber, requestCount);
409.             int pageNumber = e.PageNumber;
410. 
411.             UIElement page;
412.             bool pageReady = false;
413. 
414.             // Try to get the page if it was previously generated.
415.             lock (printSync)
416.             {
417.                 pageReady = pageCollection.TryGetValue(pageNumber - 1, out page);
418.             }
419. 
420.             if (!pageReady)
421.             {
422.                 // The page is not available yet.
423.                 page = await GeneratePageAsync(pageNumber, currentPageDescription);
424. 
425.                 // If the ticket changed discard the result since the content is outdated.
426.                 if (Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref requestNumber, requestNumber, requestCount) != requestCount)
427.                 {
428.                     return;
429.                 }
430. 
431.                 // Store the page in the list in case an invalidate happens but the content doesn't need to be regenerated.
432. 
433.                 lock (printSync)
434.                 {
435.                     pageCollection[pageNumber - 1] = page;
436. 
437.                     // Add the newly created page to the printing root which is part of the visual tree and force it to go
438.                     // through layout so that the linked containers correctly distribute the content inside them.
439.                     PrintCanvas.Children.Add(page);
440.                     PrintCanvas.InvalidateMeasure();
441.                     PrintCanvas.UpdateLayout();
442.                 }
443.             }
444. 
445.             PrintDocument printDoc = (PrintDocument)sender;
446. 
447.             // Send the page to preview.
448.             printDoc.SetPreviewPage(pageNumber, page);
449.         }
450. 
451.         /// <summary>
452.         /// This is the event handler for PrintDocument.AddPages. It provides all pages to be printed, in the form of
453.         /// UIElements, to an instance of PrintDocument. PrintDocument subsequently converts the UIElements
454.         /// into a pages that the Windows print system can deal with.
455.         /// </summary>
456.         /// <param name="sender">The print document.</param>
457.         /// <param name="e">Arguments containing the print task options.</param>
458.         protected override async void AddPrintPages(object sender, AddPagesEventArgs e)
459.         {
460.             PrintDocument printDoc = (PrintDocument)sender;
461. 
462.             // Loop over all of the preview pages
463.             for (int i = 0; i < NumberOfPhotos; i++)
464.             {
465.                 UIElement page = null;
466.                 bool pageReady = false;
467. 
468.                 lock (printSync)
469.                 {
470.                     pageReady = pageCollection.TryGetValue(i, out page);
471.                 }
472. 
473.                 if (!pageReady)
474.                 {
475.                     // If the page is not ready create a task that will generate its content.
476.                     page = await GeneratePageAsync(i + 1, currentPageDescription);
477.                 }
478. 
479.                 printDoc.AddPage(page);
480.             }
481. 
482.             // Indicate that all of the print pages have been provided.
483.             printDoc.AddPagesComplete();
484. 
485.             // Reset the current page description as soon as possible since the PrintTask.Completed event might fire later (long running job)
486.             currentPageDescription = null;
487.         }
488. 
489.         /// <summary>
490.         /// Helper function that clears the page collection and also the pages attached to the "visual root".
491.         /// </summary>
492.         private void ClearPageCollection()
493.         {
494.             lock (printSync)
495.             {
496.                 pageCollection.Clear();
497.                 PrintCanvas.Children.Clear();
498.             }
499.         }

22. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402352&clcid=0x409
23. 
24. namespace SDKTemplate
25. {
26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     public sealed partial class MainPage : Page
30.     {
31.         public static MainPage Current;
32. 
33.         public MainPage()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36. 
37.             // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance
38.             // in order to call methods that are in this class.
39.             Current = this;
40.             SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;
41.         }
42. 
43.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
44.         {
45.             // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file
46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();
47.             int i = 1;
48.             foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)
49.             {
50.                 itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $"{i++}) {s.Title}", ClassType = s.ClassType });
51.             }
52.             ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;
53. 
54.             if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
55.             {
56.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
57.             }
58.             else
59.             {
60.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
61.             }
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
66.         /// sample scenario page.
67.         /// </summary>
68.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
69.         /// <param name="e"></param>
70.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
71.         {
72.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
73.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
74. 
75.             ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;
76.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
77.             if (s != null)
78.             {
79.                 ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);
80.                 if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
81.                 {
82.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
83.                 }
84.             }
85.         }
86. 
87.         public List<Scenario> Scenarios
88.         {
89.             get { return this.scenarios; }
90.         }
91. 
92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// Display a message to the user.
94.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
95.         /// </summary>
96.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
97.         /// <param name="type"></param>
98.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
99.         {
100.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
101.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
113.         {
114.             switch (type)
115.             {
116.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
117.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
118.                     break;
119.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
121.                     break;
122.             }
123. 
124.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
125. 
126.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
127.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
128.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
129.             {
130.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
131.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
136.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137.             }
138. 
139. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
140. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
141. 			if (peer != null)
142. 			{
143. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
144. 			}
145. 		}
146. 
147.         async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
148.         {
149.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));
150.         }
151. 
152.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
153.         {
154.             Splitter.IsPaneOpen = !Splitter.IsPaneOpen;
155.         }
